
该文件汇总当前主机实测信息，以及最近一次累计 100k → 128k 双流续训的保存配置。训练已停止；本文不会启动训练或安装依赖。

关键阅读提示：实际训练解释器为 `.venv-smol5090/bin/python`；NumPy 由额外 PYTHONPATH 兼容目录提供，不能只看系统 Python 或 pip 显示的版本。当前 `pointops` 导入失败，仓库保留源码；PointROPE 与 PointOps 是两个不同扩展。训练源码显式启用 cuDNN benchmark 和 CUDA matmul TF32，不能把诊断进程的默认值当成训练值。

所有章节放在这一个文件中。敏感环境变量值已脱敏。

    "contents": "import os\nfrom setuptools import setup\nfrom torch.utils.cpp_extension import BuildExtension, CUDAExtension\nfrom distutils.sysconfig import get_config_vars\n\n(opt,) = get_config_vars(\"OPT\")\nos.environ[\"OPT\"] = \" \".join(\n    flag for flag in opt.split() if flag != \"-Wstrict-prototypes\"\n)\n\nsrc = \"src\"\nsources = [\n    os.path.join(root, file)\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src)\n    for file in files\n    if file.endswith(\".cpp\") or file.endswith(\".cu\")\n]\n\nsetup(\n    name=\"pointops\",\n    version=\"1.0\",\n    install_requires=[\"torch\", \"numpy\"],\n    packages=[\"pointops\"],\n    package_dir={\"pointops\": \"functions\"},\n    ext_modules=[\n        CUDAExtension(\n            name=\"pointops._C\",\n            sources=sources,\n            extra_compile_args={\"cxx\": [\"-g\"], \"nvcc\": [\"-O2\"]},\n        )\n    ],\n    cmdclass={\"build_ext\": BuildExtension},\n)\n"
  },
  "pointrope_source": {
    "path": "/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot-wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow/src/lerobot/policies/smolvla/litept/libs/pointrope/__init__.py",
    "exists": true,
    "bytes": 279,
    "sha256": "32ea412d88d3887397c8f7828430b5b63ae1c3093b889701195c21a62c2442d1",
    "contents": "try:\n    from .pointrope_cuda import PointROPE\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(\n        f\"[PointROPE] CUDA implementation unavailable ({type(e).__name__}: {e}). \"\n        \"Using slower Pytorch fallback.\"\n    )\n    from .pointrope_torch import PointROPE\n\n__all__ = [\"PointROPE\"]\n"
  },
  "flash_attn_imported_by_litept": true,
  "torch_scatter_imported_by_litept": true,
  "notes": [
    "SONG_POINTSEG_REQUIRE_POINTOPS=1 is a conditional path requirement, not proof of installation or invocation.",
    "The current training uses pointseg indices cache; absence of pointops alone does not prove previous training used a failing KNN/FPS path.",

```json
{
  "path": "/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot-wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow/benchmarks/song_real_libero/scripts/launch_v043_smolvlm2_2p2b_doubleflow_8x24_40k.sh",
  "exists": true,
  "bytes": 18911,
  "sha256": "00b7efa69f9532ba9ea9aaa7a4eea5bd32c2a108cabeddd42bffb97911fe731a",
  "contents": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# SmolVLA v0.4.3 double-flow training, using the existing single-flow 2.2B\n# recipe as the baseline. The intended differences are the new LIBERO dataset,\n# WorldFlow, 8 x 24 batch, and the requested 40k / 1e-4 -> 2.5e-6 schedule.\n\nWORKTREE=\"${WORKTREE:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot-wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow}\"\nPYTHON=\"${PYTHON:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot_Molmo2ER_3B/.venv-smol5090/bin/python}\"\nDATA_ROOT=\"${DATA_ROOT:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot/wep_vla_v043_multiview_data}\"\nDATASET_DIR=\"${DATASET_DIR:-${DATA_ROOT}/libero_4suite_lerobot_dataset}\"\nPOINTSEG_CACHE_DIR=\"${POINTSEG_CACHE_DIR:-${DATA_ROOT}/libero_4suite_lerobot_cache}\"\nVLM_DIR=\"${VLM_DIR:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot/SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct}\"\nNUMPY_COMPAT_DIR=\"${NUMPY_COMPAT_DIR:-/raid5/rongshengwang/.cache/lerobot-smolvla-v043-numpy126}\"\nINIT_POLICY=\"${INIT_POLICY:-}\"\n\nRUN_NAME=\"${RUN_NAME:-wep_vla_v043_multiview_doubleflow_2p2b_40k_lr1e4_to2p5e6_8x24_20260905}\"\nOUTPUT_DIR=\"${OUTPUT_DIR:-${WORKTREE}/outputs/${RUN_NAME}}\"\nRESUME_CHECKPOINT=\"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT:-}\"\nSTEPS=\"${STEPS:-40000}\"\nOPTIMIZER_LR=\"${OPTIMIZER_LR:-0.0001}\"\nSCHEDULER_WARMUP_STEPS=\"${SCHEDULER_WARMUP_STEPS:-100}\"\nSCHEDULER_DECAY_STEPS=\"${SCHEDULER_DECAY_STEPS:-30000}\"\nSCHEDULER_DECAY_LR=\"${SCHEDULER_DECAY_LR:-0.0000025}\"\nBATCH_SIZE=\"${BATCH_SIZE:-24}\"\nGRADIENT_ACCUMULATION_STEPS=\"${GRADIENT_ACCUMULATION_STEPS:-1}\"\nNUM_WORKERS=\"${NUM_WORKERS:-14}\"\nNUM_PROCESSES=\"${NUM_PROCESSES:-8}\"\nMAIN_PROCESS_PORT=\"${MAIN_PROCESS_PORT:-29670}\"\nSAVE_CHECKPOINT=\"${SAVE_CHECKPOINT:-true}\"\nSAVE_FREQ=\"${SAVE_FREQ:-2000}\"\nSAVE_STEPS=\"${SAVE_STEPS:-[100]}\"\nLOG_FREQ=\"${LOG_FREQ:-1}\"\nWORLD_SYNC_INPUTS=\"${WORLD_SYNC_INPUTS:-false}\"\nRETAIN_ONLY_LATEST=\"${RETAIN_ONLY_LATEST:-true}\"\nWANDB_ENABLE=\"${WANDB_ENABLE:-true}\"\nWANDB_MODE=\"${WANDB_MODE:-offline}\"\nRETENTION_HELPER=\"${WORKTREE}/benchmarks/song_real_libero/scripts/retain_latest_checkpoint.py\"\n\nfor required_path in \\\n  \"${WORKTREE}/.git\" \\\n  \"${PYTHON}\" \\\n  \"${DATASET_DIR}/meta/info.json\" \\\n  \"${DATASET_DIR}/libero_collect_summary.json\" \\\n  \"${DATASET_DIR}/worldflow_robot_base_upgrade.json\" \\\n  \"${POINTSEG_CACHE_DIR}/manifest.json\" \\\n  \"${VLM_DIR}/config.json\" \\\n  \"${NUMPY_COMPAT_DIR}/numpy\"; do\n  if [[ ! -e \"${required_path}\" ]]; then\n    echo \"Required path does not exist: ${required_path}\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\ndone\nif [[ ! -f \"${RETENTION_HELPER}\" ]]; then\n  echo \"Checkpoint retention helper does not exist: ${RETENTION_HELPER}\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nif [[ \"$(git -C \"${WORKTREE}\" branch --show-current)\" != \"wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow\" ]]; then\n  echo \"Wrong worktree branch: $(git -C \"${WORKTREE}\" branch --show-current)\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ \"$(git -C \"${WORKTREE}\" rev-parse HEAD)\" != \"$(git -C \"${WORKTREE}\" rev-parse remotes/origin/wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow)\" ]]; then\n  echo \"Worktree HEAD does not match the requested remote branch.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ \"${NUM_PROCESSES}\" -ne 8 || \"${GRADIENT_ACCUMULATION_STEPS}\" -ne 1 ]]; then\n  echo \"This launcher is pinned to 8 GPUs and one accumulation step.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ -z \"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT}\" && \"${BATCH_SIZE}\" -ne 24 ]]; then\n  echo \"A fresh run must use 8 GPUs x 24 samples.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ -n \"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT}\" && \"${BATCH_SIZE}\" -ne 20 ]]; then\n  echo \"An OOM resume must use 8 GPUs x 20 samples.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ \"${STEPS}\" -lt 1 ]]; then\n  echo \"STEPS must be positive.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ -n \"${INIT_POLICY}\" && -n \"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT}\" ]]; then\n  echo \"INIT_POLICY and RESUME_CHECKPOINT are mutually exclusive.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nif [[ -n \"${INIT_POLICY}\" ]]; then\n  init_policy_dir=\"$(realpath -e \"${INIT_POLICY}\")\"\n  for required in \\\n    config.json \\\n    model.safetensors \\\n    train_config.json \\\n    policy_preprocessor.json \\\n    policy_postprocessor.json; do\n    if [[ ! -s \"${init_policy_dir}/${required}\" ]]; then\n      echo \"Incomplete initialization policy: ${init_policy_dir}/${required}\" >&2\n      exit 2\n    fi\n  done\n  INIT_POLICY=\"${init_policy_dir}\"\nfi\n\n\"${PYTHON}\" - \\\n  \"${OPTIMIZER_LR}\" \\\n  \"${SCHEDULER_WARMUP_STEPS}\" \\\n  \"${SCHEDULER_DECAY_STEPS}\" \\\n  \"${SCHEDULER_DECAY_LR}\" <<'PY'\nimport math\nimport sys\n\npeak_lr = float(sys.argv[1])\nwarmup_steps = int(sys.argv[2])\ndecay_steps = int(sys.argv[3])\ndecay_lr = float(sys.argv[4])\nassert math.isfinite(peak_lr) and peak_lr > 0, peak_lr\nassert warmup_steps >= 0, warmup_steps\nassert decay_steps > 0, decay_steps\nassert math.isfinite(decay_lr) and 0 < decay_lr <= peak_lr, (decay_lr, peak_lr)\nPY\n\noutputs_root=\"$(realpath -m \"${WORKTREE}/outputs\")\"\noutput_parent=\"$(realpath -m \"$(dirname \"${OUTPUT_DIR}\")\")\"\nif [[ \"${output_parent}\" != \"${outputs_root}\" || \"$(basename \"${OUTPUT_DIR}\")\" != \"${RUN_NAME}\" ]]; then\n  echo \"OUTPUT_DIR must be an immediate child of ${outputs_root} named ${RUN_NAME}.\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\nif [[ -z \"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT}\" ]]; then\n  if [[ -e \"${OUTPUT_DIR}\" ]]; then\n    echo \"Refusing to reuse existing output directory for a fresh run: ${OUTPUT_DIR}\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\nelse\n  if [[ ! -d \"${OUTPUT_DIR}\" ]]; then\n    echo \"Resume output directory does not exist: ${OUTPUT_DIR}\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  resume_dir=\"$(realpath -e \"${RESUME_CHECKPOINT}\")\"\n  checkpoint_root=\"$(realpath -e \"${OUTPUT_DIR}/checkpoints\")\"\n  if [[ \"$(dirname \"${resume_dir}\")\" != \"${checkpoint_root}\" || ! \"$(basename \"${resume_dir}\")\" =~ ^[0-9]{6}$ ]]; then\n    echo \"Resume checkpoint is outside the exact run checkpoint root: ${resume_dir}\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\n  for required in \\\n    pretrained_model/config.json \\\n    pretrained_model/model.safetensors \\\n    pretrained_model/train_config.json \\\n    training_state/optimizer_state.safetensors \\\n    training_state/training_step.json; do\n    if [[ ! -s \"${resume_dir}/${required}\" ]]; then\n      echo \"Incomplete resume checkpoint: ${resume_dir}/${required}\" >&2\n      exit 2\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\nulimit -n 65535\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=\"${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7}\"\nexport PYTHONPATH=\"${WORKTREE}/src:${WORKTREE}:${NUMPY_COMPAT_DIR}${PYTHONPATH:+:${PYTHONPATH}}\"\nexport PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1\nexport HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\nexport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1\nexport TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM=false\nexport SONG_POINTSEG_REQUIRE_POINTOPS=1\nexport SONG_POINTCLOUD_GRIPPER_POINTS=500\n# The 8x24 double-flow step peaks near the 32 GiB device limit. json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndata_root, dataset, cache = map(Path, sys.argv[1:])\ninfo = json.loads((dataset / \"meta/info.json\").read_text())\nsummary = view.\nassert manifest[\"cache_mode\"] == \"indices\"\nassert manifest[\"camera_views\"] == [\"agentview\"]\nassert manifest[\"camera_view_fusion\"] == \"legacy_budget\"\n\nexpected = info[\"total_episodes\"]\ncontracts = {\n    \"point_clouds\": \"episode_*.zarr\",\n    \"point_clouds_robot0_eye_in_hand\": \"episode_*.zarr\",\n    \"world_base_ee_poses\": \"episode_*.npy\",\n    \"world_base_action_target_ee_poses\": \"episode_*.npy\",\n}\nfor dirname, pattern in contracts.items():\n    count = sum(1 for _ in (dataset / dirname).glob(pattern))\n    assert count == expected, (dirname, count, expected)\n\nmtime = data_root.stat().st_mtime\nprint(\n    \"Data contract OK: today's transferred LIBERO v0.4.3 multiview data; \"\n    f\"episodes={expected}, frames={info['total_frames']}, tasks={info['total_tasks']}, \"\n    \"two point-cloud views present, robot-base achieved+commanded WorldFlow present; \"\n    f\"transfer_mtime={mtime:.0f}.\"\n)\nPY\n\n\"${PYTHON}\" -c \\\n  'import numpy as np; from cumm import tensorview as tv; assert np.__version__ == \"1.26.4\"; tv.from_numpy(np.array([1], ]]; then\n  echo \"GPU compute processes are already running; refusing to overlap.\" >&2\n  nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=gpu_uuid,pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv,noheader >&2\n  exit Checkpoint config retains synchronization\n# for both training inputs and every inference denoising step.\nWORKTREE=\"${WORKTREE:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot-wep_vla_v0.4.3_multiview_doubleflow}\"\nPYTHON=\"${PYTHON:-/raid5/rongshengwang/Lerobot_Molmo2ER_3B/.venv-smol5090/bin/python}\"\nSOURCE_RUN=wep_vla_v043_multiview_doubleflow_2p2b_from70k_plus30k_lr1e4_to3e5_8x24_20260906\nINIT_POLICY=\"${WORKTREE}/outputs/${SOURCE_RUN}/checkpoints/030000/pretrained_model\"\nRUN_NAME=\"${RUN_NAME:-wep_vla_v043_multiview_doubleflow_2p2b_from100k_plus30k_sync_lr1e4_to1e5_8x24_20260907}\"\nOUTPUT_DIR=\"${WORKTREE}/outputs/${RUN_NAME}\"\n\n\"${PYTHON}\" - \"${INIT_POLICY}\" \"${WORKTREE}/outputs/${SOURCE_RUN}.train.log\" <<'PY'\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\n\np=Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve(strict=True)\nassert p.parent.name=='030000'\nfor name in ['config.json','train_config.json','model.safetensors','policy_preprocessor.json','policy_postprocessor.json']:\n    assert (p/name).stat().st_size>0,name\nt=json.loads((p/'train_config.json').read_text())\nc=json.loads((p/'config.json').read_text())\nstate=json.loads((p.parent/'training_state/training_step.json').read_text())\nassert state['step']==30_000 and t['steps']==30_000\nassert t['batch_size']==24 and t['gradient_accumulation_steps']==1\nassert t['resume'] is False\nassert c['worldflow_enable'] and not c['se3_enable']\nassert c['worldflow_noise_coupling']=='left_compose_ego'\nassert c['worldflow_world_eef_velocity_mode']=='base_pose9_euclidean'\nassert c['worldflow_action_fusion']=='point_action_expert_conjugate_bridge'\nassert c['max_action_dim']==10\nassert '2.2B' in c['vlm_model_name']\nassert 'End of training' in Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text()\nassert (p/'model.safetensors').stat().st_size>5_000_000_000\nprint(f'Validated cumulative-100k parent policy: {p}',flush=True)\nPY\n\nexport WORKTREE PYTHON INIT_POLICY RUN_NAME OUTPUT_DIR\nexport STEPS=30000 BATCH_SIZE=24 NUM_PROCESSES=8 GRADIENT_ACCUMULATION_STEPS=1\nexport OPTIMIZER_LR=0.0001 SCHEDULER_WARMUP_STEPS=0\nexport SCHEDULER_DECAY_STEPS=30000 SCHEDULER_DECAY_LR=0.00001\nexport WORLD_SYNC_INPUTS=true\nexport SAVE_CHECKPOINT=true SAVE_FREQ=2000 SAVE_STEPS='[100]' RETAIN_ONLY_LATEST=true\nexport LOG_FREQ=10 MAIN_PROCESS_PORT=\"${MAIN_PROCESS_PORT:-29681}\"\nexport WANDB_MODE=offline WANDB_ENABLE=true\nunset RESUME_CHECKPOINT\nexec bash \"${WORKTREE}/benchmarks/song_real_libero/scripts/launch_v043_smolvlm2_2p2b_doubleflow_8x24_40k.sh\"\n"
}

Keep the original endpoint-derived velocity target.\n+            x_t = self._synchronize_worldflow_inputs(ego_x_t, current_pose)\n+\n         if independent_world_trajectory or ego_x_t is None:\n             path_conjugacy_error = torch.zeros_like(noise_conjugacy_error)\n         else:\n@@ -7824,11 +7843,19 @@ class VLAFlowMatching(nn.Module):\n                             if self.worldflow_branch.action_dim == 10\n                             else projected_world_pose\n                         )\n+                    # Keep the original World accumulator for diagnostics;\n+                    # only the denoiser input is replaced. This is equivalent\n+                    # to the audited sync_all inference intervention.\n+                    world_denoiser_x_t = (\n+                        self._synchronize_worldflow_inputs(x_t, current_ee_pose)\n+                        if self.config.worldflow_sync_inputs\n+                        else world_x_t\n+                    )\n                     v_t, world_v_t = self.denoise_step_world_ego(\n                         prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\n                         past_key_values=past_key_values,\n                         ego_x_t=x_t,\n-                        world_x_t=world_x_t,\n+                        world_x_t=world_denoiser_x_t,\n                         timestep=time_tensor,\n                         world_scene=world_scene,\n                         lang_tokens=lang_tokens,\n"
    }
  ],
